1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the sr object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 250. The moduli space has dimension at most 152 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to sr theory, we decompose the problem into 250 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 152. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the sr structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 929.
